In the second group the shaft has an insufficiently definite terminal tick for resolution. There is no intervening additional Pi-Delta-r-prime to Pi-Delta-r expression.

Blind audit: The six ringed points, six additional ray marks, central mark, two separate small right-hand marks and three outer terminal marks remain individually drawn. The second count of thirteen counted the main interior marks and omitted the additional exterior marks from that subtotal. Root also sees three small white apertures in filled interior marks, the short curve just right of the center, a lower retrace along the right radius, a return across the upper-right ring, and a lower wing at the remote upper-right terminal; these are added as actual contours. The first drawing retains the downward curl at the heavy top edge, explicit dots and gaps, pale construction fragments, central 0\|o, and endpoint uncertainty. No generic completion of the faint strokes is introduced. Crop: `crops/148_scan009_second_B06.png`; original box [148, 888, 575, 1373]. | medium |

The touching pair has continuous pale equators and asymmetric red point positions. The left meridian sphere has two explicit left equatorial points and a gapped heavy equator. The central rear equatorial arc is heavy and broken around c/d, not a uniform dotted arc. The upper/lower paired spheres have short connectors and distinct solid/dotted coloured paths. The far-right pencil sphere has layered transverse contours and irregular short marks. Selected tetrahedral edges are heavy; the lower rising internal edge is broken and pale. C omits/smooths several of these strokes. No comparison to an unavailable H drawing file is claimed. Additional inspected crops: drawing_upper, drawing_left, drawing_centre, drawing_lower. ### Remaining doubts

| Place | Reading | Alternatives | Confidence | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| L01, first relation | \unsure{=\|\simeq} | =; \simeq | medium | The horizontal components are retraced and the upper component has curvature. The crop does not establish whether that curvature belongs to a comparison form or to retracing of equality. 148_scan020_formula_I.png [55,591,334,689] |
| L01, broad letter and earlier stroke state | \unsure{S_Q\|I_Q} | S_Q; I_Q; unresolved earlier letter state | low | The broad curved top and bottom admit S, while an interior upright and dark overlap also admit I. No complete independent earlier glyph is exposed. 148_scan020_formula_I.png [55,591,334,689] |
